It just feels like the day is already ruined before I even put on my work badge. So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever eat an amount of food that is much larger than what most people would eat in a short period of time?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I just... I don't have the appetite for it.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you've lost control while eating?\n\nPatient: No... I don't. Like I said, I barely eat as it is. So, I guess it's not normal.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you would enjoy eating if you were having a good day?\n\nPatient: I don't know. Food just... it doesn't really matter either way. I can't really say which started first.\n\nDoctor: Do you think a part of you might be wanting to lose weight, even if it's not something you're trying to do?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so.
